       71: * **Combination of both**: The signal is fed into both a time- and a frequency-domain transformer and the outputs are combined\n        72: * **Patch-wise attention**: Similar to the vision transformer, it extracts rectangular patches from the spectrogram, so attention happens both in the time and frequency domain simultaneously.\n        Okay, lines 69-72.\n\n    *   *Wait, check line
